## R8-2 (Fable ruling, 2026-09-15): two-tier scheme replaces uniform waiver

Fable reviewed 8e0c767a and found the uniform OPEN_SLOT_WAIVER fix (all 542
items using dictionary-form-verb/bare-particle distractors) violates Jin's
standing rule: distractors must be same POS/same conjugated form,
semantically-INCOMPATIBLE (>=2/3), with ungrammatical-form distractors as a
last resort only (low learning value -- trivially eliminable by grammar
alone, not vocabulary knowledge).

**Tier A (default, 366/542 = 67.5%)**: same-POS/same-form distractors from
a semantically clashing category -- concrete cross-domain nouns
(food/clothing/color/body/household/position/numbers) for A1-B2 items,
cross-topic abstract-register nouns for C1-C2 items, cross-topic same-
ending predicates/adverbs for verb/adjective/adverb answers. Verified by a
full sentence-by-sentence read of all 542 items (chunks of 50, logged
below) -- e.g. Fable's own flagged example `cloze_c2_0173` ("검침기
＿＿＿으로 요금이...") now reads 읽씹/비용/과장 (leaving-message-on-read/
cost/exaggeration) instead of 팔다/빌리다/에게.

**Tier B (OPEN_SLOT_WAIVER, last resort, 170/542 = 31.4%, under the 40%
cap)**: used only where the read found NO selectional restriction at all
(bare existential/generic-adjective/copula frames: "＿＿＿가 있어요.",
"다양한 ＿＿＿이 있어요.", "정말 ＿＿＿.", bare topic-fronted subjects) --
every noun/adverb-class substitution tried there (including the R8 pass's
own "abstract noun" and "closed-class number/position-word" attempts)
still read as a fully valid alternate sentence.

**Mixed (6/542 = 1.1%)**: 2 Tier-A + 1 OPEN_SLOT_WAIVER distractor, used
when the read found only 1-2 safe same-ending clashing candidates
(`cloze_a1_0314`, `cloze_a1_0443`, `cloze_a1_0188`, `cloze_a1_0251`,
`cloze_a1_0275`, `cloze_a1_0276`).

### Mechanical-audit fixes made during this pass

- `same_ending`'s bare "다" catch-all tightened further is unaffected;
  what broke was `gather_verbadj_candidates` using a looser
  `a_sig in ending_signatures(word)` containment check instead of the
  audit's own strict `same_ending()` -- fixed to call `same_ending()`
  directly so generation and audit always agree.
- A personal/temporal topic-fronting subject phrase (저는/제 친구는/
  오늘은/...) pulled from ANOTHER live cloze item's own answer is now
  globally excluded from the cross-item predicate candidate pool (not
  just the 8 originally-hardcoded pronoun prefixes) -- this is exactly
  the D7 leak the R8 sample caught in `cloze_a1_0314`.
- Bare adverb-answer items ("저는 지금 ＿＿＿ 가요.", "저희 가게에
  ＿＿＿ 오세요!") default to Tier B -- cross-domain adverb swapping
  (unlike cross-domain NOUN swapping) reliably produced valid alternates
  in the read (항상/다시/꼭/천천히 all fit equally well).
- A bare noun coincidentally ending in the "-고" connective's own syllable
  (연고/신고/채용 공고/...) no longer misroutes to the predicate-form
  candidate pool -- scoped narrowly to "고" only (not "다"/"요", which are
  overwhelmingly genuine predicate markers, not noun tails) after this
  fix, applied identically in both the candidate generator and the audit,
  broke a batch of genuine conjugated-phrase answers (같이 웃다,
  인사드리겠습니다) the first time it was tried too broadly.

Full 542-item Tier A/B/mixed assignment and per-item read is in
`docs/data/c2c_cloze_distractor_changes.csv` (tier column) and the R8 full
read table above (superseded per-item where a later override changed the
pick -- the changes CSV is authoritative for the FINAL state).

Re-running the mechanical audit after all R8-2 fixes: **0 violations**
(D1/D2/D3/D4/D6 all 0). `test_audit_cloze_distractors.py`: 27/27 pass,
including 2 new `MixedTierTests`/`OpenSlotWaiverTests` cases covering the
"고"-noun misrouting fix and the mixed-composition acceptance logic.
## R8-2 full read -- final state, every changed item, sentence-by-sentence (2026-09-15)

All 542 items that changed in the C2c sweep, in their FINAL (post-R8-2) state. Tier A items use same-POS/same-form semantically-incompatible distractors (judged: reads grammatical but doesn't hold in context); Tier B/mixed-waiver-part items use OPEN_SLOT_WAIVER (judged: grammatically impossible in the slot). See docs/data/c2c_cloze_distractor_changes.csv for the tier of each item.
